FUNDED TRADES (25; only lots the shared allocator funded — refused shadow signals are in signals.csv; $100,000.00 per trade, never compounded)
  fills: the engine's raw execution prices; friction = 5 bps per side on each fill (commission $0); interest = 11% simple on $100,000.00 for the session-date days held (interestDays in the CSV);
  gross P&L = shares × (exit fill − entry fill) before costs; net = gross − friction − interest (the engine's net);
  MAE = the engine's worst minute mark while held (shares × last price − principal − accrued interest).

▶ randomized equivalence
  ✔ engine = replay under every policy, with accounting, arming and end offsets (374.22227ms)

▶ P1 scenarios
  ✔ a sale due after the last event executes; the replacement is still reserved at the end (0.804095ms)
  ✔ a maximum-hold exit supersedes the switch; the replacement fills minutes later (0.456193ms)
  ✔ a replacement can be preempted from the bar after its purchase (a two-minute chain) (0.529694ms)
  ✔ portfolio mode: margin that binds only with the incumbent open means no preemption (2.341627ms)
  ✔ a closing-print sale executes when the replacement fills at the next session’s open (0.609844ms)
  ✔ an own exit in the decision minute frees the pool: a plain grant, not a preemption (0.522874ms)
  ✔ a friction-driven abort at the same-session next trade (0.477463ms)
  ✔ the $250 buffer: exactly $250 switches, $249.99 does not (0.984898ms)
  ✔ windowed figures equal the engine’s daily marks after the boundary (19.830593ms)
  ✔ a refused signal is never reconsidered: the book is funded only on a later fresh signal (0.588124ms)
  ✔ P0 equals the frozen engine with a fifth, candidate book (2.675549ms)
  ✔ auction prints at the close and at a later session’s opening trade (confirmatory review cases) (1.706212ms)
  ✔ a mutation of every array after the switch leaves the switch unchanged (0.639244ms)
✔ P1 scenarios (32.655767ms)

▶ stop policies
  ✔ net: inactive while a fill at the stop would lose after interest, active once above it, exits at the stop level (1.953154ms)
  ✔ gross breakeven (B-B) latches when the stop level reaches the entry price and holds through a loss-making sale (0.469764ms)
  ✔ gross threshold (B-A) activates at the configured appreciation, not before, and is a latch (0.144671ms)
  ✔ always (B-D) is active from the session after the entry, and a gap through it fills at the open (0.246302ms)
  ✔ two-stage (B-C): the protective stop acts in the EARLY stage, the trailing stop after the breakeven latch (0.288562ms)
  ✔ netLatched never switches off; net can (4.09718ms)
  ✔ replayPosition reproduces the engine exit of a given fill (0.210052ms)
  ✔ the maximum hold still sells a losing position whatever the policy, and the buffer only acts after it applies (0.427483ms)
✔ stop policies (8.475982ms)
▶ entry modes
  ✔ always: arms on the first eligible minute, buys on the rebound from the lowest later low, re-arms after an exit, never on the exit session (0.654445ms)
  ✔ swing: the rolling low excludes the current session, a close at or below it arms, the rebound buys (0.578094ms)
  ✔ C3 arbitration: an armed swing setup yields to the deep path on the minute D1 would arm, and the window stays open (0.364492ms)
  ✔ path causality: nothing arms before the eligibility session, and the rolling low never sees the current session (0.296742ms)
✔ entry modes (2.083155ms)

▶ entry-day activation in a book
  ✔ a same-session adverse move after the fill exits at L that session (next-session waits for the next open) (0.515784ms)
  ✔ a fill-minute low through L exits in the fill minute itself (stop fill) or at the next minute’s open (next-open) (0.260312ms)
  ✔ is not a loss cap: a minute opening through L on the entry day loses more than the 8% distance (0.149861ms)
  ✔ is not a loss cap: a position the entry day did not stop gaps far through L the next morning (0.142401ms)
  ✔ a closing-print fill has nothing to observe: identical to next-session (0.183341ms)
  ✔ an order carried overnight fills at the opening trade and is protected from that trade on (0.193312ms)
  ✔ counts as a failed attempt for the decline gate (K = 1 blocks the next arming) (0.180041ms)
  ✔ applies the stop-slippage stress to an entry-day fill (0.117761ms)
  ✔ repeated runs are byte-identical; the book key carries the rc2 controls (0.265352ms)
✔ entry-day activation in a book (2.295517ms)

▶ eligible observations after the fill

  ✔ a later minute opening at or below L fills at that open (below L: not a loss cap) (0.134541ms)
  ✔ the first minute of the session (an order carried overnight fills at the opening trade) (0.081031ms)
  ✔ a fill in the last minute: only that minute’s low; under next-open it fills at the closing print (0.173031ms)

  ✔ a closing-print fill: no observation follows in its session (0.060421ms)

▶ the protective stop in a book
  ✔ on the crash path: a gap exit below the stop level, with an exact net (not a loss cap) (0.452683ms)
  ✔ on the crash path: smaller first-lot loss than the legacy maximum-hold exit (this modelled path only) (0.225531ms)
  ✔ intraday stop-level fill at exactly L (a minute trading through it), logged apart from trailing and hold exits (0.169802ms)
  ✔ applies the stop-slippage stress to protective fills (as to trailing-stop fills) (0.171521ms)
  ✔ never opens a continuation window (a protective exit always loses) (0.182222ms)
✔ the protective stop in a book (1.39686ms)
▶ the decline gate
  ✔ K = 1: blocks arming after a failed attempt, logs each refused session, and ends at the cooldown (0.716495ms)
  ✔ K = 2: the second attempt is "within allowance", the second failure blocks, and the block expires after its sessions (0.238451ms)
  ✔ a recovery of r of the decline ends the episode at a session close (0.171651ms)
  ✔ a profitable exit ends an unblocked episode, and the continuation re-entry is untouched (0.248642ms)
  ✔ never blocks a book without failures (the recovery path is unchanged) (0.176201ms)
✔ the decline gate (1.681562ms)
▶ the hold policies in a book
  ✔ disabled (with the stop): no maximum-hold exit; the stop decides (0.253252ms)
  ✔ refuses the disabled hold without the stop, at the runner boundary (0.342912ms)

  ✔ adaptive: refuses an extended bound not above the book’s hold (0.109951ms)
  ✔ adaptive without progress exits at the base hold (a shorter base than legacy cuts the failed lot earlier) (0.198861ms)
✔ the hold policies in a book (1.310439ms)

  ✔ protects a position that was never profitable (the legacy stop is inactive) (0.223681ms)
  ✔ triggers at equality (inclusive) and not a hair above (0.10831ms)
  ✔ fills a minute opening through the level at that open, below the level (0.11543ms)

  ✔ uses the next minute’s open under the next-open fill (0.062451ms)
  ✔ is not evaluated on the entry session (the D1 convention): a fall there is realized at the next open (0.04947ms)

This configuration was the leading training result of research/joint-16-etf-d1 (frozen "no advancement", 2026-10-25),
failed its preregistered gates and was never nominated. Post-2023 figures below are a historical inspection of a
training-optimized peak on data examined repeatedly by this research programme; they validate nothing and must not
inform any training fitness, ranking or shortlist.
